## 2026-05-10 Minimum Raise Parity Fix

- Timestamp: 2026-05-10T18:23:21Z
- Type: audit_fix
- Failure class: action_mapping
- Summary: Slumbot diagnostics showed live action-mapping drift for under-minimum fractional raises. CPU full-deck, fast-state, CUDA, Slumbot play, and range-tracker legal masks now require each fractional raise bucket to meet the no-limit minimum raise contribution before it is legal.
- Verification: `pytest -q test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_eval.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_slumbot.py test/unit/test_slumbot_diagnostics.py test/unit/test_network_mask.py test/unit/test_slumbot_mapping.py test/unit/test_legal_mask_parity.py`; `/usr/bin/env NUMBA_ENABLE_CUDASIM=1 python scripts/test_feature_encoding.py`; direct CUDA-sim legal-mask probe.

## 2026-05-10 GPU Training Readiness Fix

- Timestamp: 2026-05-10T18:36:07Z
- Type: audit_fix
- Failure class: compute_bottleneck
- Summary: Numba CUDA compilation failed because NVVM was not discoverable from the active Python env. Installed `nvidia-cuda-nvcc-cu12==12.6.85`, added the missing `libnvvm.so.4` compatibility symlink, and added `scripts/cuda_env.py` so GPU scripts set `CUDA_HOME` plus `NUMBA_FORCE_CUDA_CC=8.6` inside Python before importing Numba.
- Verification: `pytest -q test/unit/test_cuda_env.py test/unit/test_train_slumbot_args.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_eval.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_slumbot.py test/unit/test_slumbot_diagnostics.py test/unit/test_network_mask.py test/unit/test_slumbot_mapping.py test/unit/test_legal_mask_parity.py`; one-iteration Slumbot GPU training smoke saved `models/autoresearch_minraise_smoke/corrected_minraise_final.pt` with `device=cuda`, `traverse=2.3s`, `train=0.6s`.

## 2026-05-10 GPU Replay Cache OOM Fix

- Timestamp: 2026-05-10T18:42:00Z
- Type: audit_fix
- Failure class: compute_bottleneck
- Summary: The 300-iteration corrected-minimum-raise training gate failed at iteration 13 with CUDA OOM after the 10M replay buffer pushed GPU cache usage near the 8GB card limit. Added a memory-budget guard so `_MultiBufferView` skips the GPU replay cache when its estimated tensor footprint would consume too much free GPU memory.
- Verification: `pytest -q test/unit/test_gpu_cache_budget.py test/unit/test_cuda_env.py test/unit/test_train_slumbot_args.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_eval.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_slumbot.py test/unit/test_slumbot_diagnostics.py test/unit/test_network_mask.py test/unit/test_slumbot_mapping.py test/unit/test_legal_mask_parity.py`.

## 20260511T140726Z-compact-gpu-replay-cache-reduces-memory-budget - passed

- Timestamp: 2026-05-11T14:07:26Z
- Type: audit_fix
- Failure class: compute_bottleneck
- Summary: Added compact GPU replay-cache storage for features and advantages, keeping iterations in fp32 and promoting compact advantage targets back to fp32 in the loss path. The GPU trainer now releases traversal workspace before training and releases replay cache after training so PyTorch cached memory does not starve the next Numba traversal. A reuse-budget guard keeps full-cache rebuilds off when the per-iteration training draw is much smaller than the replay buffer.
- Metrics: Compact replay-cache estimate for 20,000,000 samples is 5.104 GiB versus 10.133 GiB with float32 feature/advantage tensors. Direct CUDA probe returned `features=torch.float16`, `advantages=torch.float16`, and `iterations=torch.float32`. A 1,000,000-sample synthetic replay benchmark with 125 batches of 8,192 samples measured 0.235s host fallback versus 0.065s compact cache (`3.629x` faster). A 30-iteration Slumbot-track training benchmark crossed the 20,000,000-sample buffer mark without CUDA OOM and held steady at roughly `traverse=1.6-1.7s`, `train=1.0-1.1s`, `iter=2.8-2.9s` after warmup.
- Verification: `pytest -q test/unit/test_gpu_cache_budget.py test/unit/test_cuda_env.py test/unit/test_train_slumbot_args.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_eval.py test/unit/test_poker_autoresearch_slumbot.py test/unit/test_slumbot_diagnostics.py test/unit/test_network_mask.py test/unit/test_slumbot_mapping.py test/unit/test_legal_mask_parity.py`; `/usr/bin/env NUMBA_ENABLE_CUDASIM=1 /home/max/miniconda3/bin/python scripts/test_feature_encoding.py`; `/home/max/miniconda3/bin/python scripts/train_slumbot_2p.py --n-iterations 3 --n-traversals 100 --n-training-steps 50 --buffer-capacity 200000 --hidden-dim 128 --n-layers 2 --batch-size 512 --save-dir models/autoresearch_compact_cache_smoke_20260511T1401Z --prefix compact_cache_smoke --eval-every 0 --save-every 0`; `/home/max/miniconda3/bin/python scripts/train_slumbot_2p.py --n-iterations 30 --n-traversals 800 --n-training-steps 500 --buffer-capacity 10000000 --hidden-dim 512 --n-layers 4 --batch-size 2048 --save-dir models/autoresearch_compact_cache_benchmark_20260511T1416Z --prefix compact_cache --eval-every 0 --save-every 0`.
